# Report 6 — 3D Project Showroom — Full Engineering Spec (Plan Mode Only)
No code, no deploy, no asset commits. Every "render" or "model" must carry provenance. Flags used: REQUIRES_OFFICIAL_ASSET, OFFICIAL_SOURCE_REQUIRED, LEGAL_REVIEW, NEEDS_VERIFICATION. Every performance number = נדרש אימות בבדיקה ידנית / Lighthouse / WebPageTest.
This module is NadLan's defensible moat: a real interactive 3D model per project, tied to a verified apartment table, hotspot-linked, AR-capable on mobile, and **provenance-honest**. Benchmarks: Matterport (captured-reality dollhouse), Sketchfab (universal glTF viewer), Zillow 3D Home (consumer capture), Compass 3D Showcase. We are not building a "pretty render"; we are building a verifiable spatial index of the project.
---
## 1. State Machine (the contract)
Every project page renders the Showroom in exactly one of three states. The state is computed server-side from the project record and is **non-overridable** by the CMS user.
text
                      ┌──────────────────────────┐
   has_official_3d?── │  OFFICIAL                │
        │  yes  ──►   │  badge: "מודל רשמי"      │
        │             │  source line required    │
        │             └──────────────────────────┘
        │ no
        ▼
   has_concept_3d? ── ┌──────────────────────────┐
        │  yes  ──►   │  CONCEPT                 │
        │             │  badge: "הדמיה רעיונית"   │
        │             │  watermark + disclaimer  │
        │             └──────────────────────────┘
        │ no
        ▼
                      ┌──────────────────────────┐
                      │  MISSING                 │
                      │  badge: "אין מודל זמין"  │
                      │  no viewer, only CTA     │
                      └──────────────────────────┘

### 1.1 OFFICIAL
- **Trigger**: asset uploaded with provenance.type ∈ {developer_official, rmi_official, municipality_official} AND provenance.document_url present AND LEGAL_REVIEW=approved.
- **Badge**: green chip, label מודל רשמי · {source name}, links to provenance document in a new tab with rel="noopener".
- **Copy under viewer**: מודל זה סופק על-ידי {מקור} בתאריך {date}. עודכן לאחרונה {updated_at}.
- **Allowed**: hotspots linked to verified apartment rows, floor switcher, day/night, AR, fullscreen, share.
- **Forbidden**: any decorative element not in the source file (no added trees, no fake skyline, no staged furniture unless it ships from the developer).
### 1.2 CONCEPT
- **Trigger**: asset uploaded with provenance.type = concept (in-house, agency, or speculative).
- **Badge**: amber chip, label הדמיה רעיונית — לא מחייבת.
- **Visual treatment**: persistent diagonal watermark הדמיה רעיונית at ~8% opacity across the canvas; bottom banner inside the viewport (not removable, not behind a toggle).
- **Copy under viewer**: הדמיה זו נוצרה לצורך המחשה בלבד ואינה מהווה מצג מטעם היזם או הרשויות. הנתונים הרשמיים מופיעים בטבלת הדירות ובמסמכי התב"ע.
- **Allowed**: rotate, zoom, generic floor labels.
- **Forbidden**: hotspots that imply a specific apartment is "this exact unit"; AR mode (concept-in-AR misleads); any "official" iconography; price overlays on the 3D itself.
### 1.3 MISSING
- **Trigger**: no asset.
- **Render**: no viewer canvas. A neutral card with project axonometric line-drawing placeholder (SVG, not a render), badge אין מודל זמין, and two CTAs: בקש מהיזם מודל רשמי (lead form, tagged request_3d) and קבל עדכון כשהמודל יעלה (email capture).
- **Forbidden**: stock 3D, AI-generated building, "coming soon" renders that look like the real building.
### 1.4 Transitions
Concept → Official only by re-upload with valid provenance; never by editing flags. Official → Missing only by takedown ticket + audit log entry. All state changes are logged with actor + timestamp.
---
## 2. Model Data Pipeline
### 2.1 Formats
- **Primary web**: .glb (binary glTF 2.0). Single file, embedded textures, easiest CDN.
- **iOS AR Quick Look**: .usdz sibling, same basename. Required for iOS Safari AR (<a rel="ar">).
- **Android Scene Viewer**: same .glb (Scene Viewer reads glTF).
- **Source of truth in storage**: .glb + .usdz + original DCC file (.blend/.max/.ifc) kept private for re-export.
- **Rejected**: .fbx, .obj, .dae for delivery (no web standard, large, no PBR consistency).
### 2.2 Optimization budget (per project model)
All values are **targets**, נדרש אימות בבדיקה ידנית:
- Triangles: ≤ 250k for building exterior, ≤ 500k for exterior + ground floor interior, ≤ 1.2M for full interior walkthrough (LOD-gated).
- Texture atlas: ≤ 4096² PBR set (basecolor/normal/ORM), KTX2 + Basis Universal compression.
- Geometry: **Draco** compression on positions/normals/UVs (meshopt acceptable alternative; pick one project-wide, not both).
- File size targets: exterior-only .glb ≤ 6 MB, full project ≤ 18 MB, .usdz ≤ 25 MB. Hard ceiling: 35 MB; above it the asset is rejected at upload.
- LOD: minimum 3 levels (LOD0 close, LOD1 mid, LOD2 silhouette). Switching distance defined per model in the asset manifest, not hardcoded.
### 2.3 Delivery
- **CDN**: served from the same edge as listing imagery; Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable; filename includes content hash.
- **Lazy-load**: viewer JS + model bytes load only when the Showroom section enters viewport (IntersectionObserver, rootMargin 200px). Above-the-fold poster image (WebP/AVIF) ships in the initial HTML.
- **Range requests**: CDN must support Range so model-viewer can stream large .glb.
- **Preconnect** to model CDN in <head>; **no** <link rel="preload"> for the model itself (would defeat lazy strategy).
- **Decoder assets**: Draco/Basis decoders pinned to a specific version, served from the same origin to avoid third-party fetch on first paint.
### 2.4 Asset manifest (per project)
Stored alongside the model, drives the viewer:
text
{
  project_id, state, provenance{type, source_name, document_url, captured_at, uploaded_by, approved_by},
  files{glb_url, usdz_url, poster_url, decoder_base},
  camera{default, presets[{name, position, target}]},
  hotspots[{id, position, normal, link_type: apartment|floor|amenity|poi, link_id}],
  floors[{id, label_he, label_en, y_min, y_max, apartment_ids[]}],
  lighting{day_preset, night_preset, hdri_url?},
  bounds{min,max}, units: "meters",
  legal{watermark: bool, disclaimer_he, disclaimer_en}
}

---
## 3. Viewer
### 3.1 Library choice
- **Default**: <model-viewer> (Google, web component, glTF + USDZ + AR built in, ~80 KB gz, accessible defaults, RTL-neutral).
- **Escalate to three.js / R3F only when** the project needs: custom shader, animated cutaways, synced multi-camera, or hotspot occlusion that model-viewer can't express. Escalation requires a written justification on the project record; otherwise stay on model-viewer for consistency and bundle weight.
- **Never**: Babylon, PlayCanvas, or a second viewer in parallel — one library per project type.
### 3.2 Controls (mandatory)
- Orbit (drag), pan (two-finger / shift-drag), zoom (wheel / pinch), with clamped min/max radius derived from bounds.
- Reset-view button (returns to camera.default).
- Camera presets dropdown sourced from camera.presets (e.g. "חזית", "כניסה", "גג", "חתך אנכי").
- Floor switcher: vertical stack of floor chips, RTL-aligned to the inline-start edge; selecting a floor isolates apartments on that floor in the linked table and dims hotspots elsewhere.
- Day/Night toggle: swaps HDRI + exposure preset; persisted per session in localStorage key nadlan:showroom:lighting.
- Fullscreen: Fullscreen API, with ESC + on-screen close.
- Share: copies a deep link that encodes ?cam={preset}&floor={id}&hotspot={id} so a shared link reopens the exact view.
### 3.3 Hotspots
- Anchored to model coordinates from the manifest, not screen-space.
- Three link types: apartment (opens apartment drawer + scrolls the apartment table to that row), floor (switches floor), poi (opens map drawer focused on that POI).
- Hotspot DOM nodes are real buttons with aria-label, focusable, keyboard-activatable.
- In CONCEPT state: only floor hotspots allowed.
### 3.4 AR
- Mobile only (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference AND coarse pointer AND model-viewer reports AR available).
- Button label: צפה בדגם במציאות רבודה.
- Disabled in CONCEPT state.
- iOS: USDZ via Quick Look. Android: Scene Viewer with mode=ar_preferred.
---
## 4. Provenance
Every asset row carries:
- type ∈ developer_official | rmi_official | municipality_official | concept.
- source_name (free text shown to users).
- document_url (PDF/permit/letter; required for any non-concept).
- captured_at (date the source data reflects).
- uploaded_by, approved_by (internal user IDs).
- legal_review_status (pending|approved|rejected) — LEGAL_REVIEW gate before the asset can ship in OFFICIAL state.
Rendered as a "Provenance" line directly under the viewer in all states. In CONCEPT and MISSING the line explicitly says no official source exists. **Forbidden**: hiding the provenance line behind a tooltip, or showing it only on hover. It is body copy, not chrome.
OFFICIAL_SOURCE_REQUIRED is enforced at upload — the form will not submit an OFFICIAL asset without a document_url.
---
## 5. Page Modules (the Showroom page composition)
Order is fixed; modules hide (not reorder) when data is missing.
1. **Hero band**: project name, address, status chip (תכנון/בנייה/אכלוס), official badge.
2. **Showroom canvas** (this spec).
3. **Provenance + disclaimer line** (always visible).
4. **Floor tour**: horizontal scroller of floor cards, each opens the floor in the viewer.
5. **Apartment table**: verified rows (unit no., rooms, sqm, floor, price if public, status). Each row links bidirectionally with a hotspot. Only this table — never the 3D — is the source of truth for apartment data.
6. **Map + POI panel**: split with the existing map module; POIs are official datasets only (OFFICIAL_SOURCE_REQUIRED).
7. **Planning timeline**: תב"ע / היתר / תחילת בנייה / אכלוס צפוי, every row sourced.
8. **Developer card**: links to developer hub, other projects, public record.
9. **Lead form**: phone + name + preferred contact window. LEGAL_REVIEW for consent copy. In MISSING state the same form is the only CTA.
10. **Gallery**: photos + renders; each image labeled צילום or הדמיה — never mixed without labels.
---
## 6. Performance, Fallbacks, Accessibility
### 6.1 Performance targets (נדרש אימות)
- LCP (poster image) ≤ 2.0 s on mid-tier mobile, 4G.
- Showroom JS + decoder ≤ 180 KB gz combined, lazy-loaded.
- First model byte fetched only after intent (viewport intersection OR explicit טען מודל תלת-ממד button on Save-Data / 2G).
- CLS = 0: canvas reserves a fixed aspect ratio (16:10 desktop, 4:5 mobile) before model loads.
### 6.2 No-WebGL / failure fallback
- Detect webgl2 then webgl support; on failure show the poster image, the floor list, and the apartment table — the page still functions without the viewer.
- Error states: model_failed_to_load, model_too_large_for_device, ar_unavailable. Each has its own short Hebrew copy and a retry action.
### 6.3 Accessibility
- The viewer must not be the only path to any data. The apartment table, floor list, and POI list expose the same information in DOM.
- All controls are real <button>s with aria-label; focus ring visible; keyboard: arrows orbit, +/- zoom, 0 reset, F fullscreen, Esc exits.
- Hotspots reachable by Tab in DOM order matching floor order.
- prefers-reduced-motion: disable auto-rotate and camera intros.
- Provide a non-3D alternative link: מעבר לתצוגה נגישה ללא תלת-ממד that anchors to the apartment table.
---
## 7. RTL and Mobile
- All chrome uses logical CSS properties (inset-inline-start, padding-inline). Floor switcher sits on the inline-start edge in both LTR and RTL builds, which means visually right in Hebrew.
- Directional icons (chevrons in preset dropdown, share arrow) mirror in RTL; the 3D scene itself does not mirror.
- Mobile layout: canvas 4:5, controls collapse into a bottom action bar (סיבוב | קומות | יום/לילה | AR | מסך מלא). Apartment table becomes a bottom sheet drawer summoned from a דירות בפרויקט pill.
- AR button only on mobile; on desktop the slot shows סרוק QR לצפייה ב-AR בנייד.
- Touch targets ≥ 44×44.
---
## 8. SEO and Schema
The crawler sees no WebGL. It must still understand the project.
- The Showroom section is wrapped in semantic markup: <section aria-labelledby="showroom-h"> with a real <h2> "סיור תלת-ממד בפרויקט {name}".
- The poster image has descriptive alt including project name and "מודל תלת-ממד".
- Apartment table, floor list, and provenance line are server-rendered HTML — never injected only by client JS.
- **Schema**:
  - RealEstateListing / Residence per apartment row (existing project schema).
  - Place for the project with geo, address, containedInPlace (neighborhood, city).
  - ImageObject for poster with contentUrl and creditText = source name.
  - 3DModel (schema.org) for the asset: encodingFormat: "model/gltf-binary", contentUrl: glb_url, creditText, license (from provenance), isBasedOn linking to the provenance document.
  - In CONCEPT state, 3DModel carries disambiguatingDescription: "הדמיה רעיונית" and is **not** marked as image of the Residence.
  - sameAs on the project entity links to the developer's official project page and to any RMI / municipality record.
- Sitemap: project URLs only; do not list raw .glb files in the sitemap.
- robots: model CDN paths Disallowed from indexing (no point indexing binary assets).
---
## 9. Sketchfab Integration (bidirectional)
Sketchfab is a **fallback + distribution channel**, not the source of truth.
- **Embed inbound**: when a developer's official model is hosted on Sketchfab and the upload provides a Sketchfab model URL with verified ownership, we embed via the Sketchfab Viewer API inside the same canvas slot. The state machine still applies; the embed wears the same badge and provenance line. Sandbox the iframe (allow="autoplay; fullscreen; xr-spatial-tracking", referrerpolicy="no-referrer").
- **Embed outbound**: every OFFICIAL model gets a public Sketchfab mirror under the NadLan org account, with a back-link to the canonical project page in the Sketchfab description and model.externalUrl field. This is for backlinks and discovery, not for primary delivery.
- **API**: use Sketchfab Viewer API to drive setCameraLookAt, listen to camera-changed, and forward hotspot annotations into our apartment table; if the API call fails, the embed degrades to a plain iframe with native controls.
- **Forbidden**: embedding third-party Sketchfab models we don't own as if they were the project's official model. Any embedded model not owned by NadLan or the developer is auto-flagged CONCEPT.
---
## 10. QA Checklist + Hard Bans
### 10.1 QA (must all pass before publish)
- [ ] State badge matches provenance.type.
- [ ] OFFICIAL has a clickable provenance document that returns 200.
- [ ] CONCEPT shows watermark + disclaimer in DOM (not just CSS background).
- [ ] MISSING shows no viewer canvas at all.
- [ ] .glb < 35 MB; .usdz present if mobile traffic expected.
- [ ] LCP poster reserves aspect ratio; CLS = 0 measured.
- [ ] Viewer JS does not load until intersection.
- [ ] WebGL-disabled browser still renders apartment table + floor list + POIs.
- [ ] All controls reachable by keyboard; focus ring visible.
- [ ] RTL build: controls on inline-start, icons mirrored, scene not mirrored.
- [ ] Hotspots in CONCEPT are floor-only.
- [ ] AR disabled in CONCEPT and on desktop.
- [ ] Apartment table is the source of truth — disabling the viewer does not hide any apartment.
- [ ] Schema validates: Place, Residence, 3DModel (with correct encodingFormat), ImageObject.
- [ ] Share link round-trips camera + floor + hotspot params.
- [ ] Sketchfab embed (if used) is sandboxed and labeled.
- [ ] LEGAL_REVIEW=approved recorded for OFFICIAL; consent copy on lead form reviewed.
### 10.2 Hard bans (REQUIRES_OFFICIAL_ASSET enforcement)
- A concept render shown without watermark + disclaimer.
- A concept render labeled "official", "מודל היזם", "מודל רשמי", or "מהיזם".
- AI-generated buildings used as project models in any state.
- Stock 3D buildings reused across projects.
- Hotspots in CONCEPT linking to specific apartment IDs.
- Price overlays painted on the 3D canvas itself (prices belong only in the apartment table with source).
- Hiding the provenance line behind hover/tooltip.
- Loading the model on initial page load (must be intent/intersection gated).
- Indexing .glb URLs in sitemap or linking them as canonical.
- Embedding a third-party Sketchfab model as if it were the developer's.
- Replacing the apartment table with a 3D-only experience.
---
**Verification required before any of this ships**: performance budgets via Lighthouse + WebPageTest on real mid-tier Android; legal review of every CONCEPT disclaimer and lead-form consent string; provenance documents archived in private storage with audit log; schema validated in Google Rich Results test for at least 5 sample projects across all three states.

# Report 7 — NadLan Revenue Model & Go-To-Market (Plan Mode Only)
Scope: monetization plan for every category (resale, rent, new projects, urban renewal, professionals, contractors, suppliers, commercial, land, investment, foreign investor). Numbers are directional and flagged NEEDS_VERIFICATION. Legal items flagged LEGAL_REVIEW.
---
## 1. Revenue Streams (by category)
### A. Lead generation (primary revenue, year 1–2)
Sold per qualified lead (PPL) or per accepted lead (PPA). Lead = verified phone + intent + geo + category. All forms must include כפתור "הסכמה לדיוור ולהעברת פרטים" with explicit text per חוק הגנת הפרטיות + תיקון 40 לחוק התקשורת. LEGAL_REVIEW.
| Buyer | Lead type | Directional price (₪) | Cap/month |
|---|---|---|---|
| יזם (פרויקט חדש) | קונה פוטנציאלי לפרויקט ספציפי | 180–450 | per project |
| מתווך | קונה/שוכר לפי עיר+טווח מחיר | 60–180 | per agent |
| עו"ד נדל"ן | עסקה בהליך מתקדם | 250–600 | per firm |
| יועץ משכנתאות | קונה עם prequalification חלקי | 120–280 | per advisor |
| שמאי | הזמנת שומה | 80–200 | per appraiser |
| קבלן שיפוצים/בנייה | בקשת הצעת מחיר | 40–150 | per contractor |
| ספק (מטבחים, מיזוג, אלומיניום) | פרויקט פרטי/יזמי | 60–220 | per supplier |
| מסחרי (משרדים/חנויות) | שוכר/קונה עסקי | 300–900 | per broker |
| מגרשים/קרקעות | משקיע/יזם | 400–1,200 | per listing owner |
| משקיע זר | רכישה להשקעה | 600–1,500 (USD-pegged) | per agency |
All prices NEEDS_VERIFICATION against Yad2/Madlan/WinWin benchmarks via מחקר שוק ידני.
### B. B2B SaaS
1. **Developer Dashboard** (יזמים): ניהול פרויקט, ליבת לידים, אנליטיקס ביקוש לפי עיר/טיפולוגיה, ניהול חדר תצוגה תלת-ממד, ניהול מחירון/זמינות, ייצוא לדיווח רגולטורי.
2. **Agency Workspace** (סוכנויות תיווך): CRM קל, ניהול צוות, רוטציית לידים, דשבורד ביצועים.
3. **Data Licensing API**: גישת קריאה למאגר עסקאות, מחירי שוק, ביקוש לפי שכונה. ללקוחות מוסדיים בלבד (בנקים, חברות ביטוח, קרנות, אקדמיה). חוזה + DPA.
4. **3D Hosting** (אירוח מודלים תלת-ממד): per-project או per-seat לארכיטקטים/יזמים. כולל CDN, גרסאות, embed.
### C. Promoted Listings (sponsored, transparent)
- כל יחידה ממומנת חייבת תווית ממומן בולטת + tooltip "תוצאה זו מוצגת בעדיפות תמורת תשלום". חובת גילוי לפי חוק הגנת הצרכן. LEGAL_REVIEW.
- אסור: ערבוב ממומן ב-organic ללא הפרדה ויזואלית; אסור ranking ממומן בעמוד יחיד שמוצג כ-"הכי מתאים" ללא תווית.
- מודלים: CPM (חשיפה), CPC (קליק לעמוד נכס), Featured Project Slot (חודשי, מוגבל ל-N שקופיות לעיר).
### D. Professional Profiles — Freemium + Subscriptions
- **Free**: פרופיל בסיסי, עד 5 ביקורות מאומתות, ללא לידים.
- **Pro** (₪249–₪449/חודש, NEEDS_VERIFICATION): קבלת לידים, badge מאומת, סטטיסטיקות פרופיל, עד 3 ערים.
- **Premium** (₪899–₪1,490/חודש): מספר לידים גדול, ערים ללא הגבלה, עדיפות הצגה (מסומן ממומן), 3D portfolio hosting.
- **Enterprise** (custom): סוכנויות 10+ סוכנים, API, SLA, training.
### E. 3D Asset Hosting & Production
- אירוח: ₪199–₪699/חודש לפרויקט (לפי תעבורה).
- הפקה (אם NadLan מפיק מקור רשמי): one-time ₪8,000–₪45,000 לפרויקט. OFFICIAL_SOURCE_REQUIRED.
- אסור: לגבות על הצגת מודל "concept" כאילו רשמי.
### F. Tertiary (year 2+)
- Mortgage marketplace (revenue share with יועצים), insurance comparison, moving services marketplace, urban-renewal owner-organizer toolkit (freemium → paid).

## 3. Funnel: Capture → Route → Convert → Retain
### Capture (per Report 2 — top converting pages)
1. **Listing detail page** → טופס ליצירת קשר + WhatsApp deep link → מתווך/בעלים.
2. **Project page (3D Showroom)** → "תאם ביקור" + "הורד מפרט" → יזם.
3. **City/Neighborhood page** → "קבל הצעות מ-3 מתווכים מאומתים".
4. **Renewal hub** → "האם הבניין שלך מתאים?" wizard → עו"ד/מארגן.
5. **Professional profile** → "בקש הצעת מחיר".
6. **Mortgage calculator** → "השווה 3 יועצים".
### Route (lead distribution engine)
- מאפיין: עיר, קטגוריה, טווח מחיר, דחיפות, שפה.
- חוקים: round-robin בתוך tier; קדימות ל-Premium; SLA 5 דק' לתגובה ראשונה; מי שלא ענה תוך X — הליד מוקצה מחדש (auto-reassignment).
- Anti-fraud: dedup לפי טלפון+24h, חסימת מספרים מזוייפים, OTP אופציונלי.
### Convert
- Lead → first contact: יעד ≥ 70% תוך שעה.
- First contact → meeting/quote: יעד ≥ 30%.
- Meeting → signed deal: לא נמדד ע"י NadLan ישירות; דורש דיווח חוזר (post-deal survey) → תמריץ הנחה על חידוש.
### Retain
- Pro accounts: דשבורד ROI חודשי (לידים, contact rate, פגישות מדווחות).
- Churn warning: ירידה >30% MoM ב-contact rate → CSM שיחה.
- Reactivation: הצעה חד-פעמית 50% חודש על חזרה.
---
## 4. Go-To-Market — First 100 Pros / 20 Devs / 5 Agencies
### Phase 0 (week 0–2): Foundation
- בחירת 3 ערים פיילוט: ת"א, חיפה, באר שבע (כיסוי ביקוש שונה; NEEDS_VERIFICATION מול נתוני Semrush).
- Manual data seed: 200 פרויקטים פעילים, 500 listings, 2,000 רשומות מקצוענים (from פנקס המתווכים, לשכת עוה"ד, פנקס שמאים — פתוח לציבור).
- Compliance review של כל הטפסים והעתקי השיווק. LEGAL_REVIEW.
### Phase 1 (week 2–8): 100 Professionals
- Cold outreach: 1 SDR + נכסי תוכן (Report 3 dashboards).
- ערוצים: שיחה קרה (מספרים ציבוריים מפנקסים), 
 InMail, WhatsApp Business (רק לאחר opt-in או למספרים ציבוריים — LEGAL_REVIEW נגד חוק התקשורת).
- Hook: "פרופיל מאומת חינם + 5 לידים ראשונים חינם".
- Conversion target: 8–12% מ-1,000 פניות = 80–120 פרופילים פעילים.
**Cold script (טלפון, עברית, < 45 שנ')**:
> "שלום [שם], מדבר/ת [שם] מ-NadLan, פורטל הנדל"ן החדש בישראל. ראיתי שאת/ה פעיל/ה ב[עיר]. אנחנו מציעים פרופיל מאומת חינם וחמישה לידים ראשונים על חשבון הבית, ללא התחייבות. שתי דקות לעבור על זה עכשיו או להעדיף שאשלח קישור ב-WhatsApp?"
**Email sequence (5 touches, 14 ימים)**:
1. Day 0 — Intro + 5 free leads offer.
2. Day 3 — Case study (פרופיל מקצוען מקביל, מסומן [demonstrative]).
3. Day 7 — Data point: "X פניות בעיר שלך החודש".
4. Day 11 — Founder note (אישי, קצר).
5. Day 14 — Break-up: "אסגור את הקובץ אם לא נשמע ממך".
### Phase 2 (week 4–12): 20 Developers
- Top-down: רשימת 80 יזמים פעילים (היתרי בנייה 12 חודשים אחרונים — דאטה ציבורית מ-data.gov.il).
- Direct outreach לסמנכ"לי שיווק. הצעה: 3 חודשים חינם של Developer Dashboard + 3D hosting לפרויקט אחד.
- Anchor logo strategy: לחתום 3 יזמים מובילים → להציג כ-social proof לשאר.
- Conversion target: 25% מ-80 = 20.
### Phase 3 (week 8–16): 5 Agencies
- שותפויות סוכנויות בינוניות (20–60 סוכנים). הצעה: Workspace חינם 6 חודשים + onboarding + רוטציה מועדפת על לידים פתוחים בעיר שלהן.
- מודל: revenue share על PPL לאחר תקופת ניסיון, או חבילת חודשית.
### Partnerships (year 1)
- **רשות מקרקעי ישראל**: בקשת data access ל-מכרזי קרקע (פתוח), הצגה כ-canonical source בעמודי קרקע. NEEDS_VERIFICATION of process.
- **עיריות פיילוט** (ת"א, חיפה): שיתוף נתוני תב"ע, היתרים, התחדשות. בתמורה: הצגת מידע ציבורי נגיש לתושב.
- **דאטה ציבורית**: gov.il, נסח טאבו (חיבור דרך מסלקה רשמית — חוזה), Lamas, בנק ישראל.
- **אקדמיה**: מחקר משותף עם הטכניון/אונ' ת"א — חשבון על מגדלים, ביקוש, מחירים. תוצר: מחקר רבעוני "מדד NadLan" → PR.
### PR Angles (year 1)
1. "פורטל הנדל"ן הראשון בישראל עם חדר תצוגה תלת-ממד אמיתי לכל פרויקט".
2. "מחקר NadLan: כך נראה שוק המגדלים בישראל ב-2026" (data study, רבעוני).
3. "שקיפות בנדל"ן: NadLan חושפת את מקור כל נתון".
4. עיתונאי יעד: Calcalist Real Estate, TheMarker, Globes נדל"ן, Ynet כלכלה.
---
## 5. KPIs & Unit Economics
### Acquisition
- **CAC (per Pro)**: יעד < ₪400, NEEDS_VERIFICATION.
- **CAC (per Developer)**: יעד < ₪6,000.
- **CAC payback**: Pro < 3 חודשים; Dev < 6 חודשים.
### Lead economics
- **Cost per lead generated** (organic SEO mature state): יעד < ₪25.
- **Cost per lead** (paid acquisition pilot): יעד < ₪70.
- **Lead → sold ratio**: יעד 60–75% (אחרת overproduction → churn מצד הקונה).
- **Margin per lead**: 70%+ (digital marginal cost).
### Retention & LTV
- **Pro monthly churn**: יעד < 6%.
- **Developer annual churn**: יעד < 20%.
- **LTV (Pro)**: ARPU × gross margin / churn. דוגמה: ₪350 × 0.75 / 0.06 = ₪4,375.
- **LTV:CAC**: יעד ≥ 4:1.
### Product
- Time to first lead (Pro): יעד < 7 ימים מהרשמה.
- Contact rate (lead נענה): יעד ≥ 70% תוך שעה.
- Showroom 3D engagement: יעד median session ≥ 45 שנ' על עמוד פרויקט.
### SEO (feeds revenue)
- Organic sessions, ranked keywords (per Report 1), lead share from organic ≥ 60% by month 12.
---
## 6. Israeli Compliance — Hard Rules
All LEGAL_REVIEW before launch.
1. **לשכת עורכי הדין** — איסור הבטחת תוצאות, איסור שיווק מטעה, איסור פרסום השוואתי בעייתי. עו"ד בפרופיל: לא מציגים "אחוז ניצחונות", לא "מומחה" ללא הסמכה רשמית.
2. **חוק המתווכים במקרקעין, תשנ"ו-1996** — רק מתווכים בעלי רישיון תקף יכולים להופיע כ"מתווך". מספר רישיון חובה בפרופיל. אימות מול פנקס המתווכים. אחריות NadLan: חובת בדיקה סבירה.
3. **חוק הגנת הצרכן** — תוכן ממומן מסומן בבירור ("ממומן" / "תוצאה ממומנת"). מחירים סופיים כולל מע"מ. ביטול עסקה מקוונת 14 יום למנויים.
4. **חוק הגנת הפרטיות + תקנות אבטחת מידע 2017** — רישום מאגרים, DPO, מדיניות פרטיות עברית, זכות עיון/תיקון/מחיקה. הסכמה מפורשת להעברת ליד לצד ג' עם שם הצד ומטרת השימוש.
5. **תיקון 40 לחוק התקשורת** — opt-in מפורש לדיוור; לא ניתן לשלוח SMS/Email שיווקי ללא הסכמה. כפתור הסרה בכל הודעה.
6. **חוק שוויון הזדמנויות בעבודה** — אסור פילטרים מפלים (גיל, מגזר, מצב משפחתי) בכל מנוע חיפוש (כולל professional matching).
7. **חוק שוויון זכויות אנשים עם מוגבלות** — נגישות WCAG 2.2 AA לאתר ציבורי.
8. **חוק איסור הלבנת הון** — לעו"ד/מתווכים: NadLan לא אחראית לציות הלקוח, אך לא תקדם פלטפורמת תשלום ללא KYC לאסיביליות עסקה גדולות.
9. **גילוי נאות במאמרי תוכן** — כל פוסט שיש בו אינטרס מסחרי (יזם ספציפי, פרויקט ספציפי): תווית "תוכן בשיתוף" בראש העמוד. LEGAL_REVIEW.
10. **GDPR** (לבסיס הצרכן הזר): privacy policy אנגלית, lawful basis לעיבוד, DPA עם ספקים.

## 7. Launch Sequence — Synced to Build Queue (Report 4)
| Quarter | Build (per R4) | Monetize |
|---|---|---|
| Q1 (M1–3) | City pages, listings v1, professional directory, basic project pages | Free professional profiles, PPL pilot with 10 design partners (no charge) |
| Q2 (M4–6) | 3D Showroom v1 (10 pilot projects), urban renewal hub, lead routing engine | Start PPL billing (מתווכים, עו"ד); Pro subscription launch |
| Q3 (M7–9) | Developer Dashboard, agency workspace, schema/data depth | Developer subscriptions (3 anchor logos), agency contracts |
| Q4 (M10–12) | Data API, mortgage marketplace, foreign-investor surface (EN) | Data licensing pilots, mortgage rev-share, sponsored slots GA |
| Y2 H1 | Commercial vertical, land vertical depth | Enterprise tiers, expansion to 10 cities |
Rule: לא לפתוח מונטיזציה לקטגוריה לפני שיש 3 חודשים של organic traffic מספק בעמודים הרלוונטיים (per R1 keyword targets).

## 8. Risks & Flags
| Risk | Severity | Mitigation |
|---|---|---|
| יזמים לא יספקו OFFICIAL 3D assets → showroom נשאר CONCEPT | High | חוזה data partnership עם anchor 3 יזמים; in-house 3D production for top 50 projects |
| Yad2/Madlan response (price war, feature copy) | High | Moat = 3D + data depth + schema + provenance; לא להתחרות במחיר PPL הזול |
| Lead quality complaints → churn מהיר | High | Anti-fraud + OTP + dispute mechanism + refund policy |
| תלונה משפטית מלשכת עוה"ד/מתווכים על ranking | Medium | תיעוד אלגוריתם הדירוג, תוויות ממומן ברורות, תהליך ערעור |
| Privacy authority audit | Medium | DPO ממונה, רישום מאגרים, ביקורות תקופתיות |
| תלות בכתובי קוד פתוח/דאטה ציבורית שתשנה תנאים | Medium | Multiple sources, חוזים מסחריים backup |
| Cash burn לפני CAC payback | High | Stage gates: לא לגייס SDR שני לפני LTV:CAC ≥ 3 |
Flags: LEGAL_REVIEW (כל המודולים בסעיף 6), NEEDS_VERIFICATION (כל מספרי המחיר, CAC, churn — לאמת מול benchmarks ופיילוט בפועל), OFFICIAL_SOURCE_REQUIRED (לכל אסט תלת-ממד שמחויב כ-OFFICIAL).

Now my honest read, since you asked for truth over comfort:

WordPress is the right content layer and the wrong app layer. It's ranking today — keep it for the 962 projects, articles, glossary, schema. But a map-first, 3D, logged-in, dashboarded, lead-routing, multilingual portal is a web application. The realistic end-state is two integrated systems: WordPress (content/SEO/EEAT) + a React app (search/map/3D/dashboards/auth/leads) sharing one design system. Forcing all of it into WordPress will hit hard walls. That's not a problem to hide — it's the central architecture decision, and the prompt makes o3-pro commit to it.

Lovable lock-in is avoidable, but only if you manage it. Lovable writes real React and can sync to your GitHub. You're safe if you (1) connect it to your repo, (2) own the backend/database yourself, (3) deploy to your own host. The trap is letting Lovable's hosting + default Supabase become your system of record. Also be clear-eyed: Lovable cannot see your live site or existing 3D — it builds from prompts and uploads. "Connect to the repo" means export its output to your GitHub, not Lovable ingesting nad-lan.co.il.

"Everyone goes inside every apartment in 3D" is not feasible day one — and pretending otherwise would waste your money. New projects can have developer models. Second-hand and rentals can't all have real interiors unless someone scans them (Matterport ≈ cost per unit) or the agent uploads. The honest, buildable version is tiered: Mapbox 3D city map everywhere (click a building → details), project exterior + view simulation for new projects, real interior walkthrough only where a capture exists, and a clean photo+floorplan+view fallback for everything else. Start with new projects (your real money right now), expand coverage over time.

You'll need a CRM/lead engine, and full autonomy has legal edges. WordPress is not a CRM. And in Israel, broker-license verification, consent/marketing copy, and payment KYC legally need a human checkpoint — the rest (content, publishing, SEO, social, GBP, image generation, support triage, lead routing) can run agent-only. One free build credit/day won't build this; a real build means a paid Lovable plan or — cleaner — exporting and letting a coding agent build on your own repo.

I'm holding the build credit until o3-pro returns the new prompt series, so we spend it on the right thing. Want me to also fire the short self-audit prompt (in the appendix) inside Lovable now, so its answer is waiting when you bring back the ChatGPT Pro output?

# Plan: NadLan Visual Component Gallery (15 screens)
Static visual reference only. No API calls, no auth, no real data. RTL Hebrew. All fake content tagged DEMO. Lives under one route /gallery so it doesn't pollute the eventual production site.
## What gets built
### Route structure

src/routes/gallery.tsx              → gallery index (links to all 15 screens)
src/routes/gallery.$screen.tsx      → single dynamic route renders any screen by slug

One dynamic route + a registry map keeps the file count tiny and consistent.
### Screen registry (15 entries)
| # | Slug | Screen | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | home-desktop | Homepage desktop | Hero search, city tiles, featured projects, data strip |
| 2 | home-mobile | Homepage mobile | Same content, mobile-first stack, sticky search |
| 3 | projects-archive | Projects archive | Filter rail + grid of project cards |
| 4 | project-card | Project card | Isolated card variants: official / concept / missing |
| 5 | project-showroom-official | Project showroom — official | Full hero, plans, units table, developer block, "מאומת" badge |
| 6 | project-showroom-concept | Project showroom — concept | Same shell, "קונספט" badge, disclaimers, faded data |
| 7 | project-showroom-missing | Project showroom — missing | Skeleton + "מידע חסר — עזרו לנו להשלים" CTA |
| 8 | listing-archive | Listing archive | Split map ↔ list, sticky filter bar |
| 9 | listing-card | Listing card | Isolated card with price, area, rooms, badges |
| 10 | listing-detail | Listing detail | Gallery, key facts, agent block, similar |
| 11 | city-page | City page | City hero, stats strip, neighborhoods grid, projects & listings tabs |
| 12 | pro-directory | Professional directory | Filter by vertical/city, pro card grid |
| 13 | pro-card | Professional card | Isolated, verified/unverified states |
| 14 | pro-join | Join Pro page | 3-step form (static), pricing tiers |
| 15 | developer-dashboard | Developer dashboard shell | Sidebar + KPI tiles + listings table |
### State variants per screen
Every screen exposes a toolbar with state toggles: default · loading · empty · error · disabled. Toggle is local state, no data fetching.
### Components built (shared, in src/components/gallery/)
- GalleryShell — RTL frame, screen title, state toolbar, viewport toggle (desktop/mobile preview)
- DemoBadge — small DEMO chip on every fake data block
- StateBadge — מאומת / קונספט / מידע חסר
- SearchBar — hero + sticky variants
- ProjectCard, ListingCard, ProCard, CityTile
- MapStub — static SVG of Israel coastline + pin clusters (no Mapbox/Leaflet)
- FilterRail, StickyFilterBar
- KPI, DataStrip, StatTile
- Skeleton, EmptyState, ErrorState
- RTLProvider — wraps gallery in dir="rtl" and Hebrew font
### Design tokens (from Report 5, no luxury cliché)
Defined in src/styles.css under @theme:
- **Background**: near-white oklch(0.98 0.005 240) / deep ink oklch(0.18 0.02 250)
- **Surface**: oklch(1 0 0) cards on subtle off-white
- **Primary**: confident blue oklch(0.52 0.18 245) — not corporate navy, not tech purple
- **Accent (data-positive)**: oklch(0.65 0.16 155) muted emerald
- **Accent (data-warning)**: oklch(0.72 0.14 70) amber for "concept"
- **Accent (data-missing)**: oklch(0.6 0.02 250) neutral gray
- **Border**: hairline oklch(0.9 0.005 240)
- **Typography**: Heebo (display) + Assistant (body) — both via @fontsource, loaded in src/main.tsx. No serif, no gold.
- **Radii**: tight (4/8/12px) — data-driven feel, not playful
- **Shadow**: single elevation level, low opacity
### Fake data
One file src/lib/gallery/demo-data.ts exports:
- 12 projects (4 official, 4 concept, 4 missing)
- 24 listings
- 8 cities (Tel Aviv, Jerusalem, Haifa, Netanya, Beersheba, Rishon LeZion, Petah Tikva, Ramat Gan)
- 16 pros across verticals
Every record carries __demo: true.
### Out of scope (explicit)
- No real Mapbox/Leaflet integration — static SVG only
- No forms that submit
- No auth / Lovable Cloud
- No SEO meta beyond noindex on the gallery
- No connection to Reports 2–7 keyword work
- Existing src/routes/index.tsx untouched
## After build, deliverables to you
1. **Component inventory** — printed list of every component file with its props
2. **Screenshot instructions** — exact URLs (/gallery/home-desktop, /gallery/project-showroom-concept, …) and viewport sizes (1440×900 desktop, 390×844 mobile) so you can capture all 15 screens
3. **State matrix** — table of which states are wired on which screen
## Build order (one PR-equivalent)
1. Install fonts: bun add @fontsource/heebo @fontsource/assistant
2. Tokens + RTL setup in src/styles.css + src/main.tsx
3. Shared components in src/components/gallery/
4. Demo data file
5. gallery.tsx index + gallery.$screen.tsx with registry
6. 15 screen modules in src/components/gallery/screens/
7. Verify build, output inventory + screenshot URLs
Estimated scope: ~25 new files, ~0 changes to existing files except src/main.tsx (font imports) and src/styles.css (token additions).

Thought process
Thought process
Critically examined — and this is a genuinely strong, well-engineered plan. What's right: it's isolated under /gallery with noindex and touches none of the existing routes (won't pollute production), every screen carries the full state matrix (default/loading/empty/error/disabled), all fake data is DEMO-tagged (honest), and the static MapStub avoids pulling in Mapbox for a reference build. All 15 screens match. That's the right scope for "let me see how it looks."

Two real quality catches before it builds — because the whole point is that the reference is true to Report 5:

Font violation. The plan uses Assistant for body text. Report 5 explicitly forbids Assistant ("overused"). It must be Heebo for UI/body + IBM Plex Mono for tabular numerals (prices, area, rooms). Building it with Assistant makes the visual reference wrong on day one.
Colors are approximations, not the system. The plan converts to oklch guesses ("confident blue oklch(0.52 0.18 245)"). Report 5 gives exact hex — pin them: brand 
#1561D8, ink 
#0B0F14, success 
#0E7C66, warning (concept) 
#B57700, danger (missing) 
#B5311B, bg 
#F5F7FA. Otherwise the gallery isn't a faithful token reference.
One scope note (not a blocker): the static MapStub is fine for a gallery, but flag for later that the real listings/showroom needs true Mapbox — this build is reference-only.

My recommendation: push those two corrections into the plan, then build it — this is the thing the one free credit should buy. Two things from you so I don't guess:

Did Report 3A finish and get captured before you moved to this gallery plan? And do you want me to fix the font/colors and build the gallery now (this spends the one free build credit), or hold it until 3A/3B keyword capture is done per the original order? My vote: send the 2 fixes, build the gallery now — you've wanted to see it, and the keyword capture can run in parallel.
